# Watchlist coverage

3071 companies, 1789 polled directly (58%).

- **verified** (1711): We read their job board directly and it has open jobs.
- **verified, no openings** (78): Board confirmed, nothing open right now. Checked every run.
- **own careers site** (342): Jobs live on a site we can't poll (Gem, Comeet, Dover, LinkedIn...). Their VC board's feed still catches new posts.
- **check: board name differs** (17): A board answered but its company name differs. Not polled until someone confirms it.
- **board error** (87): A board was found but errored. Retried next run; shows in the daily health check.
- **needs a careers link** (836): No job board link anywhere: not on their VC board, site or a board named after them. Mostly small companies not hiring right now. Add `careers:` in the profile for any you care about.
